| 2026-08-30 | `20260830_dr7_cementing_intermediate_deep.sql` | **DR7 Professional deep seed** — 6 modules / 26 lessons, 132 questions. Tier owns PLACEMENT. The flow path is parametrised by PUMPED VOLUME rather than depth, because depth is not single valued on a U, and pumping one cubic metre advances every front by exactly one cubic metre of path coordinate. Two maps convert back to measured depth, the annulus one running BACKWARDS from the shoe. The whole pressure calculation is one line: the maximum of zero and the annulus head plus friction less the inside head, on exact minimum-curvature TVDs, with the friction IMPORTED from the hydraulics loss kernel rather than reimplemented. The free-fall test carries a one pascal deadband, a hundred thousand times below any real deficit and a billion times above the rounding. **The tier's finding:** the rate is a straight trade, faster cures free fall and raises the circulating density at the shallowest exposed formation, and both edges are BISECTED rather than swept. On the horizontal well with a neat slurry at 1700 kg/m3 the window is CLOSED, and a lighter lead across the cased annulus opens it by moving BOTH edges. **A second:** the ECD at the CASING shoe on the horizontal well is 1837.9218199066345 kg/m3 before anything is pumped, because it divides the friction of a 1600 m lateral by 1214.859173174059 m of TVD. **A third, about the grid:** the free-fall dip is found at exactly ONE of the 61 default steps, and refining to 600 shows it spans about two cubic metres and that the coarse grid understated its depth by six percent, at -110300.25230836123 Pa rather than -104394.27505085245. | 2026-08-30 (applied alone, in ladder order; tier count verified 132) |

| 2026-09-03 | `20260903_dr9_perfsand_beginner_deep.sql` | **DR9 Associate deep seed** — 6 modules / 26 lessons, 132 questions. Tier reads the two inputs and computes neither the skin nor the sanding onset. **The finding is a CONVENTION:** a grain size curve quoted as percent RETAINED puts D10 at the COARSE end, so it comes out LARGER than D50, the reverse of what a passing curve trains. Reading one as the other swaps the two percentiles and ruins the uniformity with them, producing three wrong fields and no error anywhere; the check is the single comparison D10 > D50. **A second:** every interpolation between sieves is on a LOG scale, because a straight line on linear axes sits ABOVE the curve, so a linearly interpolated grain size is too COARSE and the gravel sized from it is too coarse with it, which is the unsafe direction. **And a refusal:** where the stack does not reach a percentile the engine returns nothing rather than extrapolating. | 2026-09-03 (applied alone, in ladder order; canary 132 questions, 6 modules, 90 module and 42 final) |
| 2026-09-03 | `20260903_dr9_perfsand_intermediate_deep.sql` | **DR9 Professional deep seed** — 6 modules / 26 lessons, 132 questions. Tier owns the four Karakas and Tariq components, the nine gun catalog at one fixed rock, and the productivity ratio. **The sign is where it earns its keep:** a negative perforation skin is not an accounting artefact, because a tunnel a third of a metre long reaches into rock an open hole of the same radius never touches, so the effective wellbore radius is genuinely larger than the drilled one. What it does NOT tell you is whether the well is damaged: minus two under a drilling damage of plus six tests as plus four, and only one of those terms is computed here. **The tier refuses to predict a well test,** which measures the SUM of every near-wellbore effect and cannot separate them. **One catalog row leaves the correlation's development range and it is the best looking row in the table,** so a tier reporting it without the warning would be recommending the one gun whose number it is least entitled to. | 2026-09-03 (applied alone, in ladder order; canary 264 cumulative, 2 structures) |

Tier owns gravel sizing, screen gauge selection, Kirsch sanding onset and the interval sweep. **THE ENGINE DEFECT THIS COURSE FOUND, TAUGHT IN m05:** `cdpAlongInterval` walked its sweep under a `md <= bottomMdM` guard, which exits before the clamp inside the loop can fire, so on any interval whose step does not divide it evenly the deepest metres were never screened; 2450 to 2550 at 25 m stopped at 2540. On an interval whose base is the weak rock that is the row that GOVERNS, and dropping it flips the reported margin positive. **Why the golden could not see it, which is worth more than the bug:** the fixture exercised only the evenly dividing case AND the oracle transcribed the engine's own loop rather than deriving the stations it should visit, so it agreed with the engine precisely where the engine was wrong. Fixed in the vendored engine and in the Suite as PR #339 (live in production), with four guards: an engine test, a ragged golden case, an oracle that now walks an EXPLICIT station list, and an app-level step guard. **The Expert capstone exercises the fix on purpose:** 2200 to 2320 at 25 m visits 2200, 2225, 2250, 2275, 2300 and a CLAMPED 2320, six rows of which the last did not exist before, and the governing and bottom CDPs are DIFFERENT numbers because the TOP governs. | 2026-09-04 | `20260904_pd4_rodpump_course.sql` | **PD4 Rod Pump Design joins the catalog, the FOURTH Production & Artificial Lift course.** Catalog row (`module='production'`; `path_order` 33; `prereq_slug` 'nodal') plus three capstones, eighteen graded fields. **A beam pump is a WAVE MACHINE**: the polished rod moves a stroke at the surface, the string carries it down as a travelling wave, and the plunger moves a DIFFERENT stroke, so almost every number a designer wants is a property of the card the wave makes rather than of the geometry that started it. FOUR RESULTS, every one verified against the engine. (1) **The static rule and the wave equation disagree by about a tenth of the stroke and the whole of the gap is inertial OVERTRAVEL.** `Sp = S - Fo*Er` is the first equation in every rod pump text and it knows nothing about a string still moving when the polished rod turns round; on the capstone it is 10.83 in low, 10.51 percent of its own answer and 8.92 percent of the surface stroke. **Both sides are graded in the SAME tier**, so a learner who stops at the spring model loses one field and keeps the other. (2) **THE REPORTED LOADS ARE NOT THE LOADS COMPUTED.** `predictCard` marches 4228 steps a cycle and accumulates the tension envelope over every one, then DECIMATES the surface card to 184 points and reads both polished rod loads off that subsample: the peak comes back 403.39 lb low and the minimum comes back **2.83 TIMES** the load the march computed, because a minimum is where a coarse sample is least likely to land on the extreme. `runRodPumpDesign` exposes NEITHER `cardSamples` NOR `nodes`, so a studio user gets the subsampled pair and no way to ask for the other, which is exactly why both are graded at the shipped default. (3) **The loads are not converged and the plunger stroke is:** across 120, 240, 480 and 960 nodes the stroke moves 0.041 in, four hundredths of one percent, while the peak moves 967.98 lb and the minimum 1108.07 lb, and at 240 nodes the march returns `notPeriodic`. The consequence stated plainly in the seeds: a worst rod loading of 105.16, 106.34, 104.60, 109.06, 111.23 percent across a 10.6 to 12.2 spm sweep is NOT monotonic and **the dip is smaller than the grid spread on the load it is built from**, so it is numerical noise and not a speed at which the rods unload. (4) **A diagnostic re-reading the card its own march produced does not get the same answer back**, 0.0813 in on the plunger stroke and 5.53 percent on the peak pump load, and both halves are graded rather than hidden. **WHAT THE ORACLE RECORDS IS NOT A DEFECT LIST BUT AN INDEPENDENCE DISCIPLINE**, and its gates hold the wave equation to 2 percent on plunger stroke and 3 percent on minimum load, so **everything smaller is invisible to it by construction** and the 2.83x minimum sits inside a 3 percent gate because the gate is on a different well. An oracle passing and an engine being right are two statements. **The capstone is OBAGI-27**, a 6085 ft three-way taper whose only fault is the rods: the unit is inside every rating it has at 74.90 / 89.38 / 84.29 percent, the barrel fills 0.853 which is three thousandths ABOVE the `incompleteFillage` cliff so a pump fifteen percent short raises no warning, the 3/4 section goes into compression at -3623.60 psi, and a structural unbalance of 810 lb with a 14 degree crank offset leaves `runRodPumpDesign` BIT IDENTICAL while moving `balanceUnit`'s peak torque 4.04 percent. Status deliberately `coming_soon`. | 2026-09-04 (rollback-wrapped dry run then applied alone; canary 102 capstones, 33 catalog rows, path_order 33 with prereq nodal) |
| 2026-09-04 | `20260904_pd4_rodpump_beginner_deep.sql` | **PD4 Associate deep seed** - 6 modules / 26 lessons, 132 questions. Tier owns THE STRING AND THE GEOMETRY, the six things a designer settles before anything moves. **Closed form is not the same as certain**, and two of the six have a plausible wrong answer beside the right one. A stepped string has TWO notes and both are returned in one object under names one letter apart, so a design checked against the uniform fundamental is checked against a string that was never run. And the textbook stroke, twice the crank radius times the front arm over the rear, is a SMALL ANGLE APPROXIMATION nobody labels as one: it is 9.93 in short on the teaching unit, eight percent of the stroke, because a pitman of finite length is not a rigid link of zero mass. | 2026-09-04 (rollback-wrapped dry run then applied alone, in ladder order; canary 100 active structures, 13198 questions) |

Three fields come from `balanceUnit`, which cannot be reached without first solving the card; one from the modified Goodman line; two from the Gibbs harmonic solver, which shares no code path with the march that made the card, so the gap between them measures the engine against itself. **An input being ACCEPTED is not an input being READ:** `runRodPumpDesign` destructures a structural unbalance, a crank offset and a kinematics object, lists all three in its own documented input list, and references none of them, which is provable by STRICT EQUALITY rather than by tolerance. And a misconception the module header itself names: the counterbalance effect is the moment over the TORQUE FACTOR a quarter turn past the bottom of the stroke, not over the beam's front arm, and using the arm understates it by roughly a factor of two. | 2026-09-04 | `20260904_pd5_gaswell_course.sql` | **PD5 Gas Well Performance joins the catalog, the FIFTH Production & Artificial Lift course.** Catalog row (`module='production'`; `path_order` 34; `prereq_slug` 'nodal') plus three capstones, eighteen graded fields. PD1 already owns the gas IPR and the node, so **PD5 is the DELIQUIFICATION course**: the liquid the gas cannot carry. FOUR RESULTS, every one verified against the engine. (1) **A well can PASS AT THE GAUGE and LOAD AT THE SHOE**: the capstone reads a ratio of 1.2552 at the wellhead and 0.9253 at the shoe, and the profile crosses one between 4,210 and 6,315 ft, so liquid is falling back over the bottom third of the string while every surface number says the well is healthy. (2) **A correlation is chosen ONCE, at the WELLHEAD, and then used everywhere.** `recommendCorrelation` takes one argument and switches at 1000 psia; the capstone wellhead is 999.62 psia, so the well gets the UNADJUSTED equation by four tenths of a psi, and that choice is then applied at a shoe sitting at 2.26 times the limit. On this well it is the difference between a 2.750 in workover string and a 2.323 in one. The message also calls whatever pressure it is handed a "wellhead". (3) **A sizing returns one answer and silently discards the rest**: the rejected 2.922 in candidate misses by 2.99 percent and never appears in `largestUnloaded`, which gained an `ok` key at engines `5733550` precisely because a null had been carrying two meanings. (4) **The plunger screen is BLESSED BY THE RULE OF THUMB AND REFUSED BY THE PHYSICS**, `pressureOk` true and `glrOk` false, the heuristic asking 3,368 scf/bbl against a force balance asking 2.763 times that. **THREE FINDINGS RECORDED AND NOT FIXED, because plungerLift feeds a live Suite app:** the slug term carries the rounded 0.433 psi/ft where the oracle uses rho g, 1,162 tolerances apart and GRADED here; the cycle gas requirement FALLS as the casing weakens so `glrOk` turns TRUE on a well that cannot move the plunger at all; and `maxSlugLengthFt` clamps to zero rather than refusing, which is a refusal wearing a number. Status deliberately `coming_soon`. | 2026-09-04 (rollback-wrapped dry run then applied alone; canary 99 capstones, max path_order 34, 34 catalog rows) |

| 2026-09-04 | `20260904_pd5_gaswell_intermediate_deep.sql` | **PD5 Professional deep seed** - 6 modules / 26 lessons, 132 questions. Tier owns THE DESIGN. The controlling station is the SHOE, which is exactly the station the gauge cannot see, and the reason is that the two quantities move in OPPOSITE directions with depth: the critical rate rises as the gas densifies and the actual velocity falls as the same standard rate occupies less volume, so **the margin closes downward on every gas well** and the only question is whether it closes before the shoe. The tier must not overstate its own answer: the design is correct GIVEN its correlation, and the correlation is a judgement made at the wrong station. | 2026-09-04 (rollback-wrapped dry run then applied alone, in ladder order; canary 98 active structures, 12934 questions) |

Only the annulus loss reaches the rock; the equivalent circulating density divides by TRUE VERTICAL depth, which is why a lateral raises it so sharply and why measured depth is an error in the unsafe direction; it is a PROFILE and the formation that fractures is not always the deepest; cuttings are worth about 11.6 kg/m3 per percent by volume in this mud and are absent from the pressure calculation, biasing it low and most at low rate, which partly cancels the friction term. A friction FACTOR is not a coefficient of friction: it absorbs genuine sliding friction, the stiffness soft string discarded, cuttings beds and every other omission, so its TREND is informative while its VALUE is not a material property. Which factor matters depends on where the side force is, so a cased-hole factor on a well whose shoe sits in the vertical section multiplies zero. Rotating REDIRECTS friction rather than reducing it, and the moment arm is the TOOL JOINT radius: using the pipe body understates the torque by 24.53 percent, which is exactly the size of error that gets mistaken for a friction problem. Side force peaks near the TOP of a build, not at total depth. Calibration is bisection over a monotone response and it ALWAYS succeeds, which is what makes it useful and what makes it dangerous, so the discipline is fit one observation and predict another. | 2026-08-30 (applied alone, in ladder order; tier count verified 132) |

Two buckling limits whose ratio is a constant; limits that are ZERO in a vertical hole, RISE with inclination and are HIGHER in a tighter hole, which is why washouts are a buckling concern. Two capacities checked with no interaction term: tension utilization scales with the string's true vertical depth and torsion with its contact length, so the two INVERT between a vertical well and a lateral by more than a factor of twenty in the ratio. Casing wear as the same side force spent over rotating hours, whose sliding distance depends only on the PRODUCT of rpm and hours (so reducing rpm is nearly useless) and whose volume-to-depth conversion is a crescent inverted by bisection, so doubling the wear factor raises the depth by about 1.6 rather than 2. Lock-up as a positive feedback the model structurally cannot predict, producing a HARD limit on lateral length. Two sharp results: past a sealing fault the late semilog line reports **45.25748634072017 mD** where the early line gives 81.25445414895721 and the truth is 85, with the skin inverting again from an entirely different cause; and a fractured well read as radial reports **26.266915078269914 mD on 5 mD rock** with a skin of minus 4.3726416175327625. **This tier found a real defect in the classifier and ships with its fix**: two ordering rules (recharge does not recover; bilinear flow precedes radial and linear flow) remove eight false labels across the seven fixtures and leave every genuine regime alone, and the ONE they cannot reach, a storage roll-off called linear flow, is taught as the case that needs the pressure curve rather than the order. The closed rectangle is the one place a well test measures a volume, and it recovers the planted area to 0.0039 percent. | 2026-08-30 (applied alone, in ladder order; tier count verified 132) |

It ends before a discount rate is mentioned. **The joint venture ledger mixes two bases:** `applyJV` scales revenue, costs and depreciation INSIDE the regime, so every row reports gross revenue, volumes, opex, capex and depreciation at FIELD level beside a royalty, taxable income, tax and net cash flow that are the working-interest SHARE (AKATA 2029 gross revenue 186032000.00 at every interest from 100 to 25 percent while royalty falls 27904800.00 to 16742880.00 at 60 percent). **The trap:** depreciation is a deduction and not a cash flow, so a shorter `jv_psc_depr_years` moves NPV without moving a single cash flow. **The engine fact:** government take is field pre-take value minus the contractor's SHARE net cash flow, so below 100 percent working interest the other partners' share counts as take (66.1723 percent at 100, 79.7034 at 60). It ends knowing that IRR is a property of a curve, not of a project. **On the real basis with the escalators set, NPV does not move with inflation:** AKATA reports 72534830.66 at 0, 1, 2, 3, 5 and 8 percent inflation while its real total net cash flow falls from 141637829.18 to 83912631.29, because Fisher deflates the flows and the rate together. | 2026-09-19 | `20261013_gv_gasvalue_beginner_deep.sql` | gasvalue ASSOCIATE DEEP SEED: six modules, 26 lessons, 132 questions (15 per module bank plus a 42 question final exam), the structure row generated from the tier's manifest.json. What this tier owns: THE GAS AND THE FLARE. A standard cubic foot is a count of moles, so the analysis is read by the mole, and the flare follows 40 CFR 98.233(n): the CO2 in the gas passes through, the hydrocarbon carbon burns at the combustion efficiency, and the methane that escapes is the methane in the gas at one less the destruction efficiency. THE ENGINE FACT: the two efficiencies set two different species and neither has a default. HELD and never graded: efficiency tiers (H1), the unlit flare (H2), GWP as a case input (H4). | 2026-09-20 (APPLIED by `apply_gv_gasvalue.sh` from the linked checkout at REF origin/main, commit 2ca181626, after `verify` matched all five pinned sha256 digests. Four routes carry requirement envelopes whose limits are the study's to set, a check with no limit is unchecked, each route's yield has a basis and a ceiling in what the gas holds, capital is scaled by the modular power law and handed on undiscounted, only the recovered share of the flare is avoided, and the breakeven credit price is closed form. THE ENGINE FACT: one flare gives three different net abatements under three counterfactuals, and the engine gives none until one is declared. Every one LOOSENS to half a unit in the place the prompt itself asks for (the FC6 rule: max(stated, half a unit of the printed/instructed precision)). `separation` beginner `ejulebe1_terminal_velocity_fts` and `ejulebe1_velocity_margin`, intermediate `ejulebe2_gas_velocity_fts`, advanced `ejulebe3_interface_height_ft`: 1e-05 to 5e-05 (prompts say "to four decimals"; 0.5342 and 3.5273 FAILED at 1e-05). `cashflow` intermediate `jv_breakeven_oil_price_usd_bbl` 0.001 to 0.005 (prompt says "to the cent"; 66.10 FAILED by 0.00101). `completion` beginner `drift_surface_casing_m` 5e-08 to 1e-07 (0.31137225 sits exactly on the 7th-decimal rounding boundary; half-up 0.3113723 FAILED by 4e-17). No expected, key, label, unit, prompt, field count or status moves. Nothing can go from pass to fail; stored attempt scores are not re-scored (the file NOTICEs attempt counts on the touched tiers). Guarded per field (published or recut tol only, expected asserted, 1 row, 6 fields); safe to re-run. Proven on a scratch replay of main 76cd1f35e: 6 updated, re-run 0, `audit.py --post` CLEAN.
